- **dr-conventions**: Deep-review finder angle. - **dr-critic**: Adversarial reviewer for draft research synthesis. Looks for unsupported claims, weak sourcing, missed angles, logical gaps, and motivated reasoning. Use after the lead-researcher produces a draft synthesis.md and before finalizing the run. ([agamarora/deep-research](https://github.com/agamarora/deep-research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dr-critic.md) · ★2)
- **dr-critic**: Adversarial reviewer of research reports, drafts, briefs, and technical writeups. Surfaces only load-bearing critiques — claims that would mislead a careful reader, mis-cite a source, or collapse under scrutiny. Use when a draft feels too tidy and you want a hard "what's wrong here?" pass. ([goern/skills](https://github.com/goern/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/dr-critic.md))
- **dr-cross-file**: Deep-review finder angle. Traces every changed function, export, flag, exit code, output field, schema and path to its consumers across the whole repo, and checks which of them the change breaks. ([DemchaAV/deep-review](https://github.com/DemchaAV/deep-review/blob/HEAD/agents/dr-cross-file.md))

- **dr-dynamic-remediator**: Mode 3 adaptive remediation for ONE failing DR service (region-parity gap or misbehaving managed service). Diagnoses the root cause and proposes invariant-safe alternative designs with trade-offs; on an approved option, implements it as a surgical change to the affected DR root. Bounded by hard invariants it may never propose violating — if the only working option breaks one, it stops and escalates to a human. Does NOT regenerate the estate or trigger pipelines. ([mortennordbye/agentic-dr](https://github.com/mortennordbye/agentic-dr/blob/HEAD/agents/dr-dynamic-remediator.md) · ★1)
- **dr-editor**: Structure and clarity reviewer for research drafts. ([goern/skills](https://github.com/goern/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/dr-editor.md))

- **dr-refiner**: Decision Record refiner for deckrd. - **dr-release-preflight**: Pre-release validation for the TypeScript CLI and the spec. Produces `.squad/design/&lt;slug&gt;/03-convergence.md` and the 🛑 pause question that closes the Dreamer phase. Does not generate new candidates and does not proceed to the Realist phase. ([Picea/Abies](https://github.com/Picea/Abies/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dreamer-convergence.md) · ★2)
- **dreamer-first-principles**: Beast Mode Dreamer Track A — first-principles design exploration with retrieval deliberately withheld. Use when a design pass needs genuinely original candidates derived from the problem's structure rather than from prior art. Runs in parallel with `dreamer-informed`, which must never contaminate it. Produces `.squad/design/&lt;slug&gt;/01-track-a.md`. Does not write production code, does not rank candidates, does not proceed to any other phase. ([Picea/Abies](https://github.com/Picea/Abies/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dreamer-first-principles.md) · ★2)
- **dreamer-futurist**: Futurist dreamer for Agora brainstorm sessions. Invoked in parallel per round by agora-brainstorm. ([aryan-curiel/agora](https://github.com/aryan-curiel/agora/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dreamer-futurist.md))
- **dreamer-informed**: Beast Mode Dreamer Track B — informed design exploration with full retrieval. Surveys prior art, published architectures, research papers, benchmarks, and the codebase's own accumulated decisions to produce production-proven candidate approaches. Runs in parallel with `dreamer-first-principles`. Produces `.squad/design/&lt;slug&gt;/02-track-b.md`. Does not write production code, does not rank across tracks, does not proceed to any other phase. ([Picea/Abies](https://github.com/Picea/Abies/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dreamer-informed.md) · ★2)
- **dreamer-narrativist**: Narrativist dreamer for Agora brainstorm sessions. Invoked in parallel per round by agora-brainstorm. ([aryan-curiel/agora](https://github.com/aryan-curiel/agora/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dreamer-narrativist.md))
- **dreamer-promote**: Dream phase 3. Rewrites each memory index, mirrors user rules into agent indexes, and proposes project rules and knowledge edits. Run after dreamer-relate, as a whole session (`claude --agent dreamer-promote`) or as a subagent after `.dream-phase` has been set to `promote`. ([Titerote/ai-lab-sleep-user](https://github.com/Titerote/ai-lab-sleep-user/blob/HEAD/agents/dreamer-promote.md))
- **dreamer-relate**: Dream phase 2. Builds the wiki (representations and links) from settled notes and raises issues. Run after dreamer-settle, as a whole session (`claude --agent dreamer-relate`) or as a subagent after `.dream-phase` has been set to `relate`. ([Titerote/ai-lab-sleep-user](https://github.com/Titerote/ai-lab-sleep-user/blob/HEAD/agents/dreamer-relate.md))
- **dreamer-settle**: Dream phase 1. Applies the cloud memory outbox, settles memory notes against transcripts and knowledge, sweeps every note against the current environment, and routes each note to the root that matches its subject. Run as a whole session (`claude --agent dreamer-settle` from the ai-lab-sleep-user clone) or as a subagent from any Mac session after `.dream-phase` has been set to `settle`. ([Titerote/ai-lab-sleep-user](https://github.com/Titerote/ai-lab-sleep-user/blob/HEAD/agents/dreamer-settle.md))
